Hmm, but wait — the dark brown cabinet at left is actually a set of drawers (the "wooden cabinet with drawers"). Actually in libero_spatial scenes, there is often a wooden box/drawer unit. The left dark object is a cabinet with handles (drawers).

Wait, but the object names include "cookies_1" — that's the cookie box. So the bowl on top of cookies_1 is the target bowl. The other akita_black_bowl sits on the cabinet? Actually looking again: the left bowl sits on top of the dark brown cabinet — that's a wooden cabinet, not cookies. Hmm, but "cookies_1" object — in libero, the "cookies" object is a cookie box which is a flat box... The bottom-center box with the Oatmeal Raisin label is the cookies box. Yes.

So target = the bowl at bottom center sitting on the cookie box. Destination = plate (bottom-right, white plate with red stripes).

Hmm wait — but the target bowl "on the cookie box". There are two black bowls. One on the cabinet top (left), one on the cookie box (center-bottom). The task says "on the cookie box" — so the center-bottom one.

Let me read memory: task-family_libero_spatial_task_t3.md and task-specific spatial_t3_s0.json.

- center pixel (515,525) → (0.029, 0.0085, z 1.0996) — this is the bowl interior floor? Actually z=1.10 at that point.
- left rim edge (520,440) → (0.0593, -0.0451, 1.120)
- right rim edge (545,610) → (0.0748, 0.0608, 1.112)
